- Removing B disconnects paths 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, and 7.
- Removing G disconnects paths 1, 2, 3, and 7.
- Removing I disconnects paths 4, 5, and 6.

- Removing B disconnects paths 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, and 7.
- Removing C disconnects paths 3, 4, 5, 6, and 7.
- Removing H disconnects paths 3, 4, 5, and 6.
